        for value in &frame.stack {
            write_value(&mut stack_data, *value);
        }
    }
    write_custom_section(&mut out, "corestack", &stack_data);

    // Prepend the Wasm header.
    let mut result = Vec::with_capacity(out.len() + 8);
    result.extend_from_slice(b"\0asm");
    result.extend_from_slice(&1u32.to_le_bytes());
    result.extend_from_slice(&out);
    Some(result)
}

/// Information about a module in the coredump.
struct ModuleInfo {
    /// A unique identifier of the module.
    id: *const (),
    /// The name of the module.
    name: String,
}

/// Captures the stack frames of the given [`Stack`].
fn capture_stack(
    stack: &Stack,
    _store: &StoreInner,
    funcs: &[CompiledCoredumpFunc],
) -> Vec<CapturedFrame> {
    let cells = stack.cells();
    let frames = stack.frames();
    let mut result = Vec::new();
    let mut next_start = cells.len();
    let mut cur_instance = stack.current_instance();

    for frame in frames.iter().rev() {
        let start = frame.start_offset();
        let meta = find_func(funcs, frame.ip_ptr());
        let (locals, locals_cells) = match meta {
            Some(meta) => (
                read_locals(cells, start, &meta.locals),
                locals_cells(&meta.locals),
            ),
            None => (Vec::new(), 0),
        };
        let locals_end = start.saturating_add(locals_cells);
        let stack_end = next_start.min(cells.len());
        let stack_len = stack_end.saturating_sub(locals_end);
        let stack = (0..stack_len)
            .map(|_| CoredumpValue::Missing)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let instance_ptr = cur_instance.map(|inst| inst.as_ptr() as usize).unwrap_or(0);
        result.push(CapturedFrame {
            instance_ptr,
            func_idx: meta.map(|m| m.func_idx).unwrap_or(0),
            locals,
            stack,
        });
        next_start = start;
        // Move to the caller instance.
        if let Some(prev) = frame.instance_opt() {
            cur_instance = Some(prev);
        }
    }
    result
}

/// Finds the coredump metadata of the compiled function containing `ip`.
fn find_func(funcs: &[CompiledCoredumpFunc], ip: *const u8) -> Option<&CoredumpFuncMeta> {
    let addr = ip as usize;
    funcs
        .iter()
        .find(|func| func.start <= addr && addr < func.end)
        .map(|func| &func.meta)
}

/// Returns the number of cells required to store a value of the given type.
fn cells_for_ty(ty: ValType) -> usize {
    if ty == ValType::V128 { 2 } else { 1 }
}

/// Returns the total number of cells required to store the given local types.
fn locals_cells(locals: &[ValType]) -> usize {
    locals.iter().copied().map(cells_for_ty).sum()
}

/// Reads the local values of a frame from the value stack.
fn read_locals(
    cells: &[crate::engine::Cell],
    start: usize,
    locals: &[ValType],
) -> Vec<CoredumpValue> {
    let mut offset = start;
    let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(locals.len());
    for ty in locals {
        let value = match ty {
            ValType::I32 => cells
                .get(offset)
                .map(|cell| CoredumpValue::I32(cell.to_bits() as i32))
                .unwrap_or(CoredumpValue::Missing),
            ValType::I64 => cells
                .get(offset)
                .map(|cell| CoredumpValue::I64(cell.to_bits() as i64))
                .unwrap_or(CoredumpValue::Missing),
            ValType::F32 => cells
                .get(offset)
                .map(|cell| CoredumpValue::F32(cell.to_bits() as u32))
                .unwrap_or(CoredumpValue::Missing),
            ValType::F64 => cells
                .get(offset)
                .map(|cell| CoredumpValue::F64(cell.to_bits()))
                .unwrap_or(CoredumpValue::Missing),
            _ => CoredumpValue::Missing,
        };
        values.push(value);
        offset = offset.saturating_add(cells_for_ty(*ty));
    }
    values
}

/// Writes an unsigned LEB128 encoded `u64` to `out`.
fn push_uleb(out: &mut Vec<u8>, mut value: u64) {
    loop {
        let mut byte = (value & 0x7f) as u8;
        value >>= 7;
        if value != 0 {
            byte |= 0x80;
        }
        out.push(byte);
        if value == 0 {
            break;
        }
    }
}

/// Writes a signed LEB128 encoded `i64` to `out`.
fn push_sleb(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: i64) {
    let mut value = value;
    loop {
        let mut byte = (value & 0x7f) as u8;
        value >>= 7;
        let sign_bit_set = byte & 0x40 != 0;
        let done = (value == 0 && !sign_bit_set) || (value == -1 && sign_bit_set);
        if !done {
            byte |= 0x80;
        }
        out.push(byte);
        if done {
            break;
        }
    }
}

/// Writes a name (LEB128 length-prefixed UTF-8 string) to `out`.
fn push_name(out: &mut Vec<u8>, name: &str) {
    push_uleb(out, name.len() as u64);
    out.extend_from_slice(name.as_bytes());
}

/// Writes a Wasm value type byte to `out`.
fn push_val_type(out: &mut Vec<u8>, ty: ValType) {
    let byte = match ty {

END STEP

pub(crate) fn handle_trap(state: &mut VmState, trap_code: TrapCode) -> ExecutionOutcome {
    let mut error: Error = trap_code.into();
    if state.store.inner().engine().config().get_generate_coredump() {
        if let Some(coredump) = crate::coredump::generate(state) {
            error = error.with_coredump(coredump);

}

        let funcs = self.funcs.lock();
        let mut result = Vec::new();

                    let start = ops.as_ptr() as usize;
                    let end = start.wrapping_add(ops.len());

/// A shared reference to the data of a [`EngineFunc`].
#[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone)]
pub struct CompiledFuncRef<'a> {
    /// The sequence of encoded [`Op`]s of the [`CompiledFuncEntity`].
    ops: Pin<&'a [u8]>,
    /// The number of stack slots used by the [`EngineFunc`] in total.
    len_stack_slots: u16,
}

impl<'a> From<&'a CompiledFuncEntity> for CompiledFuncRef<'a> {
    #[inline]
    fn from(func: &'a CompiledFuncEntity) -> Self {
        Self {
            ops: func.ops.as_ref(),
            len_stack_slots: func.len_stack_slots,
        }
    }
}

impl<'a> CompiledFuncRef<'a> {
    /// Returns the sequence of encoded [`Op`]s of the [`EngineFunc`].
    #[inline]
    pub fn ops(&self) -> &'a [u8] {
        self.ops.get_ref()
    }

END STEP

STEP 220 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && git show HEAD:crates/wasmi/src/engine/code_map.rs | sed -n '800,835p'
OBSERVATION:
returncode=0
    /// # Note
    ///
    /// This includes stack slots to store the function local constant values,
    /// function parameters, function locals and dynamically used stack slots.
    len_stack_slots: u16,
}

impl CompiledFuncEntity {
    /// Create a new initialized [`CompiledFuncEntity`].
    ///
    /// # Panics
    ///
    /// - If `ops` is empty.
    /// - If `ops` contains more than `i32::MAX` encoded bytes.
    pub fn new(len_stack_slots: u16, ops: &[u8]) -> Self {
        let ops: Pin<Box<[u8]>> = Pin::new(ops.into());
        assert!(
            !ops.is_empty(),
            "compiled functions must have at least one instruction"
        );
        assert!(
            // Generally, Wasmi has no issues with more than `i32::MAX` instructions.
            // However, Wasmi's branch instructions can jump across at most `i32::MAX`
            // forwards or `i32::MIN` instructions backwards and thus having more than
            // `i32::MAX` instructions might introduce problems.
            ops.len() <= i32::MAX as usize,
            "compiled function has too many instructions: {}",
            ops.len(),
        );
        Self {
            ops,
            len_stack_slots,
        }
    }
}


END STEP

returncode=0
    #[inline]
    fn index(&self, index: I) -> &Self::Output {
        self.as_slice().index(index)
    }
}

impl<'a> From<&'a [u8]> for SmallByteSlice {
    fn from(bytes: &'a [u8]) -> Self {
        if bytes.len() <= Self::MAX_INLINE_SIZE {
            let len = bytes.len() as u8;
            let mut buffer = [0x00_u8; Self::MAX_INLINE_SIZE];
            buffer[..usize::from(len)].copy_from_slice(bytes);
            return Self::Small { len, bytes: buffer };
        }
        Self::Big(bytes.into())
    }
}

old="""    #[inline]
    pub fn custom_sections(&self) -> CustomSectionsIter<'_> {
        self.inner.custom_sections.iter()
    }
}
"""
addition="""    #[inline]
    pub fn custom_sections(&self) -> CustomSectionsIter<'_> {
        self.inner.custom_sections.iter()
    }

    /// Returns a unique identifier of the [`Module`].
    ///
    /// # Note
    ///
    /// This is used to deduplicate modules when generating Wasm coredumps.
    pub(crate) fn unique_id(&self) -> *const () {
        Arc::as_ptr(&self.inner).cast()
    }

    /// Returns the name of the [`Module`] if any.
    ///
    /// # Note
    ///
    /// The module name is extracted from the Wasm `name` custom section.
    pub(crate) fn name(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let section = self.custom_sections().find(|s| s.name() == "name")?;
        let data = section.data();
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < data.len() {
            let id = *data.get(i)?;
            i += 1;
            let (size, n) = read_uleb(data, i)?;
            i += n;
            let end = i.checked_add(size)?;
            if end > data.len() {
                return None;
            }
            if id == 0 {
                // Module name subsection: payload is a name.
                let (len, n) = read_uleb(data, i)?;
                i += n;
                let start = i.checked_add(len)?;
                let bytes = data.get(i..start)?;
                return String::from_utf8(bytes.to_vec()).ok();
            }
            i = end;
        }
        None
    }
}

/// Reads an unsigned LEB128 encoded `u32` from `data` starting at `offset`.
fn read_uleb(data: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
    let mut result: u32 = 0;
    let mut shift = 0;
    let mut i = offset;
    loop {
        let byte = *data.get(i)?;
        i += 1;
        let low = u32::from(byte & 0x7f);
        result = result.checked_add(low.checked_shl(shift)?)?;
        if byte & 0x80 == 0 {
            return Some((result as usize, i - offset));
        }
        shift += 7;
        if shift >= 32 {
            return None;
        }
    }
}
"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old, addition)
write(p,s)

+/// Converts a Wasm trap into an [`ExecutionOutcome`], generating a Wasm
+/// coredump if coredump generation is enabled.
+pub(crate) fn handle_trap(state: &mut VmState, trap_code: TrapCode) -> ExecutionOutcome {
+    let mut error: Error = trap_code.into();
+    if state.store.inner().engine().config().get_generate_coredump() {
+        if let Some(coredump) = crate::coredump::generate(state) {
+            error = error.with_coredump(coredump);
+        }
+    }
+    ExecutionOutcome::from(error)
+}

# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is loosely based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).
Additionally we have an `Internal` section for changes that are of interest to developers.

Dates in this file are formattes as `YYYY-MM-DD`.

## `2.0.0-beta.2` - 2026-03-03

### Added

- Added `unstable` crate feature to `wasmi`, `wasmi_wast` and `wasmi_cli` crates. [#1825]
  - This enables unstable `rustc` features available in nightly Rust releases.
  - Concretely, this enables usage of Rust's unstable [`become` keyword] to enforce
    tail-calls in its instruction dispatch when `portable-dispatch` is disabled.
  - Enable `become` with `cargo build --no-default-features --features unstable`.
- Added proper `README.md` to Wasmi's CLI application. [#1824]

[`become` keyword]: https://doc.rust-lang.org/std/keyword.become.html

### Changed

- Clean-up `ResourceLimiter` API. [#1817]
  - `StoreLimits` now properly enforce panics if `trap_on_grow_failure` was enabled.
  - `memory_grow_failed` and `table_grow_failed` now take `MemoryError` and `TableError` respectively.
  - `LimiterError` is now a single variant `enum` making its use much simpler.


END STEP

STEP 232 TOOL 1
COMMAND:
cd /app && git commit -m "Add opt-in Wasm coredump generation to wasmi

Generate Wasm coredumps upon Wasm traps when enabled via
Config::generate_coredump. The executable name can be set via
Config::coredump_executable_name and defaults to an empty string.

Coredumps are only generated for Wasm traps and are attached to the
returned Error. They are accessible via Error::coredump().

The coredump is a valid Wasm binary with:
- 'core' custom section (executable name)
- 'coremodules' custom section
- 'coreinstances' custom section
- 'corestack' custom section with frames ordered youngest to oldest
- memory, global and data sections capturing the trap-time state

Locals (parameters and declared locals) are captured with their
declared types. Re-entrant Wasm calls are supported: coredump data from
an inner invocation is extended with outer frames from all active Wasm
execution levels." && git log --oneline -2
